BERT RIVERA A vaccine passport creates medical “apartheid ” creating 2 classes of people. This is not life and liberty in the USA, this is communism. Creating 2 classes of citizens based on any medical injection is illegal according to the Unruh Civil Rights Act. Other treatments are available. Medical choice is an individual choice, not a choice made by government for the individual. Any form of coercion via medical mandates, vaccine passports, QR does or any type of verification to show proof of vaccination violates Health & Safety codes #24171 “right of individuals to determine what is done to their own bodies ” and #24172(J) “Be given the opportunity to decide to consent or not to consent to a medical experiment without the intervention of any element of force, fraud, deceit, duress, coercion, or undue influence on the subject ’sdecision ”. Universally accepted codes of medical ethics including the Nuremberg Code and the Declaration of Helsinki absolutely prohibit any form of coercion to force individuals participate in a medical experiment. Big Pharma states the vaccines are voluntary and for Emergency Use Authorized only. There are many individuals who are already immune from Covid 19 as they have had the virus and recovered. There are not exceptions for these individuals further violating their rights. The manufacturers of the vaccines are exempt from liability and many individuals are getting sick and dying after being injected with the unknown substances in their drug cocktails.

73-B. Oppose Ahsram Williams Do you know what it ’s like as a dependent, to have a sole parent go into a hospital walking and talking for a minor routine procedure and be sent back on a stretcher paralyzed and unable to speak for more than a decade until death because of medical malpractice? I doubt you do. And do you know the impact and trauma of going to the hospital to be treated for a health issue that flared up only to have a medical professional improperly administer the needle and medicine which resulted in necrosis to that part of the body and necessitated prosthetic replacements? Somehow, I doubt any of you do. These are traumatic events and represent some of the things people are grappling with before making a decision. And it really doesn ’t help to say that whatever adverse reactions there may be are rare. Nobody wants to be that “rare ” case —the exception to what normally happens, especially when there is no recourse, relief or help with whatever that rarity turns out to be. If you are your sole support for yourself or your children, it is not a risk to take lightly because you, as that rare victim, and those children, are stuck with that outcome for the rest of your life. Will you be left a virtual vegetable? Will you be left requiring some sort of assisted care for the rest of your life? Where will the money for any medical attention to address it come from? These are not things to take lightly or rush headlong into simply because other people want you to do it. What one person can risk, another cannot. No one can decide for another person, what that other person can risk or not risk for his/her life. Other people aren ’t going to take care of you, and no one has the right to demand through coercion what someone else must have injected into them under penalty of the loss of their job, their housing, their livelihood. That is wrong! This item represents a cruel and beastly direction the Board wants to take and it will inflict great and inequitable harm on those communities of color who are doing the best they can just to survive. If you take away their ability to work, their liberty to go about doing things they need, to do freely, and their ability to enjoy the simple pleasures of life, you leave them with no way to live and no way out. It will result in unnecessary additional mental anguish, depression and suicides because you have taken away all hope. In effect you have taken from them their right to life, liberty and the pursuit of happiness. 

Aida Shusterman

73-B. Oppose Anna Hill Hill I am not anti-vaccine. I am deeply appreciative of the technology that has led to the eradication of especially dangerous diseases. But that does not mean that I have unqualified confidence in any vaccine; rational people can ask questions about cost/benefit analysis. Low hanging fruit example: children shouldn ’t get the vaccine, because ostensibly, for them, the risks associated with taking the vaccine are probably greater than than the danger of the disease itself. For much older adults, who are clearly at much greater risk of serious disease, this analysis is, perhaps different. Rational people can be interested in: *The categorically novel technology of m-RNA vaccines (relatively new) *The extent of testing (no especially long term studies completed yet) *Adverse reactions (appears to be rare, but sometimes very serious) *Effectiveness of the protection (breakthrough cases appear to be rare and mild) The stakes are high. Some (very few, proportionally) seem to have experienced life changing adverse reactions to taking the vaccines. Others who have not been vaccinated have died from contracting Covid. Individuals and families are faced with difficult decisions about how best to protect the health of themselves, their families, and their communities. I am trying to make the best decisions I can. I am try to be well informed, and think critically. I want to be humble about what we think we know (often less than we like to let on). All this to say, please oppose any vaccine mandates for LA County. Instead, encourage honest dialogue and promote the positive and informed decision making of my neighbors and I.

As of: 8/10/2021 4:20:24 PM PUBLIC REQUEST TO ADDRESS THE BOARD OF SUPERVISORS COUNTY OF LOS ANGELES, CALIFORNIA 

Correspondence Received 

> MEMBERS OF THE BOARD HILDA L. SOLIS HOLLY J. MITCHELL SHEILA KUEHL JANICE HAHN KATHRYN BARGER

history. THE THALIDOMIDE TRAGEDY In the 1960's, thousands were born with phocomelia as a side effect of the 

drug thalidomide, resulting in the shortening or absence of limbs. The drug was not approved in the United States in the 1960s, but as many as 20,000 

Americans were given thalidomide in the 1950s and 1960s as part of two 

clinical trials operated by the American drug makers Richardson-Merrell and 

Smith, Kline & French. THE TUSKEGEE STUDY Starting in 1932, 600 African American men from Macon County, Alabama were enlisted to partake in a scientific experiment on syphilis. The “Tuskegee Study of Untreated Syphilis in the Negro Male, ” was conducted by the United States Public Health Service (USPHS) and involved blood tests, x-rays, spinal taps and autopsies of the subjects. The goal was to “observe the natural history of untreated syphilis ” in black populations. But the subjects were unaware of this and were simply told they were receiving treatment for bad blood. Actually, they received no treatment at all. Even after penicillin was discovered as a safe and reliable cure for syphilis, the majority of men did not receive it. THE SWINE FLU LESSONS In 1976, Federal officials urged widespread vaccinations after swine flu broke out among soldiers at Fort Dix, New Jersey, killing one of the 14 diagnosed with the illness. But the program was suspended after at least 25 people died from vaccine reactions. Other estimates put the death toll at 32 people, while about 500 others later suffered from Guillain-Barre syndrome, which damages nerves and can lead to paralysis. In the aftermath, the government was criticized for pushing Americans to get unnecessary vaccinations. According to Richard Fisher of BBC, “The swine flu affair, the New York Times concluded, had been a “sorry debacle ” and fiasco marked by political expediency and unwarranted confidence. ”

Why the Hell is There Arsenic in Our Baby Food? A February 2021 report from the US House Committee on Oversight and Reform says that commercial baby foods are tainted with dangerous levels of arsenic, lead, cadmium, and mercury. The report was based on information from just four companies that make baby food: Nurture, Beech-Nut, Hain, and Gerber. Arsenic, lead, and cadmium were found in baby foods from all of the companies; mercury was found in the food from the only company that tested for it (Nurture). Yet the FDA has failed to pull these products off shelves.

Brad Hartman This serves as notice that the requirement for any individual to be vaccinated against COVID-19 for employment or participation at a university or other institution violates federal law. All COVID-19 vaccines are merely authorized, not approved or licensed, by the federal government; they are Emergency Use Authorization (EUA) only. They merely “may be effective. ” Federal law states: Title 21 U.S.C. § 360bbb-3(e)(1)(A)(ii)(I-III) of the Federal Food, Drug, and Cosmetic Act states: individuals to whom the product is administered are informed —

(I) that the Secretary has authorized the emergency use of the product; (II) of the significant known and potential benefits and risks of such use, and of the extent to which such benefits and risks are unknown; and (III) of the option to accept or refuse administration of the product, of the consequences, if any, of refusing administration of the product, and of the alternatives to the product that are available and of their benefits and risks. EUA products are by definition experimental and thus require the right to refuse. Under the Nuremberg Code, the foundation of ethical medicine, no one may be coerced to participate in a medical experiment. Consent of the individual is 

“absolutely essential. ” A federal court held that the U.S. military could not mandate EUA vaccines to soldiers. Doe #1 v. Rumsfeld, 297 F.Supp.2d 119 (2003). The court held: "...the United States cannot demand that members of the armed forces also serve as guinea pigs for experimental drugs." Id. at 135. No court has ever upheld a mandate for an EUA vaccine. The liability for forced participation in a medical experiment, including injury or death, may be incalculable. Medical and religious exemptions will be insufficient to overcome the illegality of EUA vaccine mandates. Children ’s Health Defense urges U.S. employers, universities and other institutions to respect and uphold the rights of individuals to refuse EUA COVID-19 vaccines. 

Brad Jerzykowski 

Brad Lyddon You are segregating our city by mandating this. Our black and Latino community have the lowest vaccination rate. This mandate will segregate the

Elizabeth M Zacharias Vaccination mandates and all other forms of coercion are unconstitutional, against the Nuremberg code, and a violation of each citizen's God given rights to bodily autonomy and personal sovereignty. It is foolish to think that if we hand these rights over to government authority instead of leaving them in the hands of individuals, that government medical overreach will be limited to the Covid-19 vaccines. Additionally, many of the individuals who choose not to receive the shots have robust, long-term natural immunity due to previous infection and so forcing shots is unscientific. Many others have valid medical contraindications and philosophical or religious objections, making these mandates discriminatory. If such measures pass it will prevent hard working, healthy, law abiding, and productive citizens from participating in many essential areas of society - grocery shopping for themselves and their children, participating in cultural and civic events, and on and on. It would also result in enormous losses of revenue for local businesses that are struggling to survive, and I would imagine loss of tax revenue for local government at many levels, as a significant portion of the population would transition their shopping to online and businesses in adjacent counties.

Greg Goggins 

Greg Goodman 

Greg Gore I am writing in opposition to a proposal for COVID-19 vaccine requirements for indoor public spaces. The vaccine does not prevent transmission (see "Outbreak of SARS-CoV-2 Infections, Including COVID-19 Vaccine Breakthrough Infections, Associated with Large Public Gatherings — Barnstable County, Massachusetts, July 2021," CDC, 8/6/21). Accordingly, there is no public safety benefit to limit freedom of movement based upon vaccination status. However, there are significant costs. A vaccine passport would be coercive, as it would require vaccination for freedom of movement, including potentially for the provision of basic goods and services. A vaccine passport would violate medical ethics and possibly international law, as it is inconsistent with the well established principles of voluntary, informed consent (see Nuremberg Code and Declaration of Helsinki). A vaccine passport would have a disproportionate, adverse impact on lower income, ethnic communities with relatively low vaccination rates (see "Latest Data on COVID-19 Vaccinations by Race/Ethnicity," KFF, 8/4/21). It would also have a disproportionate, adverse impact on cohorts not yet able to take the vaccine (e.g., kids under 12). Finally, a vaccine passport would discriminate against those with natural immunity based upon prior infection (see "Had COVID? You ’ll probably make antibodies for a lifetime," Nature, 5/26/21). 

Greg Hollins 

Greg Landers

Universally accepted codes of medical ethics, including the Nuremberg Code and the Declaration of Helsinki, absolutely prohibit any form of coercion to make individuals participate in a medical experiment. meeting notes and summary from the August 2020 meeting of the DHHS and CDC Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices where Dr. Cohn reminds everyone of this: “Dr. Cohn reminds everyone that under an EUA, vaccines are not allowed to be mandatory. Therefore, ...individuals must be consented and cannot be mandated to be vaccinated. ”

cdc.gov/vaccines/acip/meetings/downloads/min-archive/min-2020-08-508.pdf?fbclid=IwAR03xYf0qfZFsZ0CJT-XZx-IW4y6KhFcw5_xnRmgXY26cbspvDkbo5JxyP4 To attempt to mandate or coerce anyone into a medical treatment or procedure that is experimental violates the Nuremberg Code, the Declaration of Helsinki, and other international human rights edicts. To violate these codes, edicts, and declarations shall be considered crimes against humanity and those perpetuating these crimes shall be held accountable. Further, both Pfizer and Moderna ’s own SEC filings with the government state that all mRNA products are currently classified and considered gene therapies by the FDA. Maricela Castillo The first basic principle stated in the Nuremberg Code of 1947 says as follows: The voluntary consent of the human subject is absolutely essential. This means that the person involved should have legal capacity to give 

As of: 8/10/2021 4:20:24 PM PUBLIC REQUEST TO ADDRESS THE BOARD OF SUPERVISORS COUNTY OF LOS ANGELES, CALIFORNIA 

Correspondence Received 

> MEMBERS OF THE BOARD HILDA L. SOLIS HOLLY J. MITCHELL SHEILA KUEHL JANICE HAHN KATHRYN BARGER

consent; should be so situated as to be able to exercise free power of choice, without the intervention of any element of force, fraud, deceit, duress, overreaching, or other ulterior form of constraint or coercion; and should have sufficient knowledge and comprehension of the elements of the subject matter involved as to enable him to make an understanding and enlightened decision. This latter element requires that before the acceptance of an affirmative decision by the experimental subject there should be made known to him the nature, duration, and purpose of the experiment; the method and means by which it is to be conducted; all inconveniences and hazards reasonably to be expected; and the effects upon his health or person which may possibly come from his participation in the experiment. The duty and responsibility for ascertaining the quality of the consent rests upon each individual who initiates, directs, or engages in the experiment.... It is primarily for this basic principle that I firmly oppose any forced mandate to be vaccinated against my will as a human being fully capable of making independent decisions, free from coercion in any form, about what to allow in my body. Pam Williams In regards to agenda meeting -73-B. Evaluation of COVID-19 Vaccine Requirements for Indoor Public SpacesThe Unruh civil rights act prohibits what you are trying to allow businesses to do.Get ready for the lawsuits The Unruh Civil Rights Act (colloquially the "Unruh Act") is an expansive 1950s California law that prohibits any business in California from engaging in unlawful discrimination against all persons (consumers) within California's jurisdiction, where the unlawful discrimination is in part based on a person's sex, race, color, religion, ancestry, national origin, age, disability, medical condition, genetic information, marital status, sexual orientation, citizenship, primary language, or immigration status.[1][2][3] The Unruh Act applies to ALL businesses in California including: hotels and motels, restaurants, theaters, hospitals, barber and beauty shops, housing accommodations, and retail establishments.[4] The law was enacted in 1959 and was named for its author, Jesse M. Unruh. The Unruh Civil Rights Act is codified as California Civil Code section 51.[3][5] What do you think " medical condition " equates too? The California Supreme Court has repeatedly "interpreted the [law] as protecting classes other than those listed on its face".[6] For example, even prior to the 2005 addition of sexual orientation to the law's list of covered

Pamela Hymel I am very disappointed that Los Angeles County is even considering forcing customer vaccine mandates on public places against their will. Government should not be mandating this. Nothing even remotely close to such a mandate has ever been implemented during past pandemics, such as H1N1 in 2009. Cities could also end up forming their own public health departments in order to be exempted from county health officer jurisdiction, which would cripple and fragment public health in general as well as the response to future pandemics. Most concerningly, I have not seen any indication as to when this mandate would be lifted - or if it ever would. Eradication of COVID-19 is an impossible goal. Normalcy must eventually return, because this pandemic, like all pandemics, will eventually end, and the virus will become endemic in the same manner that H1N1 did. LA County should definitely not be imposing permanent mandates, or for that matter any vaccine mandates for public places. If the county chooses to implement a mandate anyway, then before the mandate goes into place, there should be a clear and published roadmap for ending the mandate and returning to normalcy. As the saying goes, "Know when to stop before you start." Better yet, there should be no vaccine mandates for any public places in the first place. Businesses should be allowed to decide what is right for them and their guests and employees.

Patricia J Seymour Oppose. All discussed and proposed policies to date imposed the burden of current COVID transmissions as the fault of unvaccinated individuals. Previously-infected individuals are categorically imaged as "unvaccinated", and therefore subsequently marginalized to be at fault for transmission of COVID, especially the current "Variant of Concern-Delta". Yet published studies have identified that, ""[...] results question the need to vaccinate previously-infected individuals." p. 1, see attached document. Additionally, the CDC, when referencing the Delta Variant, which is the most monitored variant in the United States at this moment, has ceased to state to the public that the available injection/vaccines will prevent infection within individuals, and are increasing including statements such as, "Fully vaccinated people with breakthrough infections from this variant appear to be infectious for a shorter period."[1] Historically, vaccination policies are constructed in order to inhibit the acquisition and the transmission of a disease. The current vaccinations are now associated with increased identifications of breakthrough infections in those who have received the injection(s), as well as identifications of periods during which vaccinated individuals who are also infected are transmitting the virus to those in their surrounding environments (regardless of vaccination status). *None* of the discussed and proposed policies to date require *all* employees, regardless of vaccination status, to undergo weekly testing. As a result and with certainty, cases will be missed of those who are vaccinated, infected with COVID, *and* transmitting COVID, passing that infection to others in the surrounding environment. It seems that the COVID-19 vaccine decreases the likelihood of death, but the likelihood of death upon contracting COVID-19 is low regardless (2%-3%) so it is hard to determine whether the low death count is the result of the COVID-19 vaccine, or because the virus already has a low death rate. 5) COVID-19 and its variants are mild viruses with a death rate of 2%-3%. Presently, it is unknown whether the risks of the vaccine outweigh the risks of COVID-19 and its variants. 6) Concerned regarding social unrest and violations of First Amendment rights. When the vaccine was made available to me in March as an employee in education, I gladly received it which was my choice and mine alone. This vaccine is under emergency use authorization and as we have seen, there are side effects for some people that we are still learning about. For some, the experimental phase of this vaccine plays a large role in the decision to put it in their body. The fear of the unknown is real for some people. It has been their choice and should remain their choice to receive it. This virus has a 98-99% survival rate yet the mass hysteria around it is astounding. We have let indecisive physicians like Dr. Fauci and the media control our beliefs and actions regarding the virus. We are surrounded by many dangerous viruses daily and have been for years, yet nothing has caused as much panic as COVID-19. Sure, it is highly contagious. Sure, some have died. Sure, some get long COVID. However, the media and the government only highlight this small percentage and tout it as “one too many.” They do not create the same mass chaos around the flu. The flu causes many deaths each year and for me, I have had what I could consider “long influenza.” Flu symptoms for me have sometimes lasted a couple months and we’re ten times worse than my experience with COVID-19. I choose the get the flu shot each year but have never been required to have it when entering a public space during flu season. As I have emphasized, vaccination with a vaccine that is under emergency use authorization should never be mandated. The Democrats have done everything but made the receiving of this vaccine mandatory through requiring 

mandatory vaccination in other areas. People are simply being bullied into making a choice they have not fully thought out. You’re attempting to control people’s own bodies which is downright wrong. This vaccine should remain a choice. If Ike choose not to vaccinate then they are making a conscious choice to take a risk. This is a risk many are willing to take for the time being and should remain this way. Stop trying to bully people to conform to the mass panic. > To: Yokomizo, Lauren; Supervisor Janice Hahn (Fourth District)
> Subject: NO VACCINE MANDATES
> Date: Monday, August 9, 2021 8:33:14 PM

I am writing in regards to possible vaccine mandates to enter into public buildings. Do we not still live in the United States of America? Are we now all considered to be sick until proven well? What about those who have had Covid and recovered and have natural immunity? 

I oppose mandatory COVID injections, Vaccine Passports and tracking and tracing apps. 

I should have the free choice to determine if I want a COVID shot. Neither government nor private corporations have the right to force me to have a so-called “Vaccine Passport.” Nor should an app that contains my private medical information and that tracks and traces my movement be permitted. 

The idea of a Vaccine Passport did not begin with COVID and will not end with COVID. Such a medical or health digital passport presents a serious threat to freedom. My private medical decisions regarding a COVID shot or other vaccine should not determine whether I can leave my home, work, shop, dine or worship. 

I stand firmly opposed to any mandatory vaccines or so-called vaccine passports. 

I urge you to fight against any direct or indirect effort to make COVID shots mandatory, to require a Vaccine Passport or to electronically track and trace my movements. 

Sincerely, Jacqueline Smith 

Please let your voice be heard on this topic: LYokomizo@bos.lacounty.gov fourthdistrict@bos.lacounty.gov The Supervisor will be introducing a motion tomorrow asking that county to report back in 2 weeks on requiring vaccinations in order for people to enter certain indoor public spaces in the County (would apply to our unincorporated areas.) 

Feel free to let us know your feedback. 73-B. Evaluation of COVID-19 Vaccine Requirements for Indoor Public Spaces 

Recommendation as submitted by Supervisor Hahn: Instruct the Director of Public Health, in concert with the Director of Consumer and Business Affairs and County Counsel, to report back to the Board within 14 days on possible options for requiring vaccines in certain indoor public spaces in the County, with the report to look at other jurisdictions that have adopted similar mandates (i.e. New York City, France, possibly City of Los Angeles), consider whether a mandate should require one dose or full vaccination and whether the policy should apply to all indoor public spaces or certain non-essential businesses and events (i.e. should grocery stores be exempt); and instruct the Director of Public Health to report back to the Board in 14 days with the process for how people can prove their vaccination status and businesses can verify vaccination status using existing digital forms of records and/or paper records. (21-3214)

Subject: Public comment submission for 8/10/2021 agenda items #21, #73-A, and #73-B 

My name is Chase Bruno, resident of Los Angeles County.  Please submit the following text in its entirety as my public comment to items #21, #73-A, and #73-B on the Board of Supervisors Agenda for 08/10/2021, since the website to do so is not functioning properly: That this is even being discussed by the board is outrageous.  No person, much less an elected official has domain over another person's body.  By considering this action, you are implying that you are qualified to make medical decisions on behalf of every single person in Los Angeles County, and your opinions supersede that of every primary care provider of your constituents. The board's decision making abilities and leadership over this past year hardly reflect the notion that you are qualified to perform the basic functions of supervisors, much less the basic functions of a healthcare professional, a job none of you remotely meet the requirement for. If your goal is to get the entirety of Los Angeles County Vaccinated, perhaps you should address the issues that make people weary of vaccination in the first place, before defaulting to tyrannical and far-overreaching measures such as mandating medical procedures for your constituents in order for them to participate in society.  Perhaps, instead of attending a dinner at a restaurant shortly after voting to ban outdoor dining, Sheila Kuehl could have ordered GrubHub and had her actions reflect her words, instead of blatantly contradicting them.  Perhaps, in doing so, you could earn back the trust of the public. Sheila Kuehl is just one example of this, I don't know that I need to name more elected who's actions have contradicted their decisions affecting the public, but we can also talk about Supervisor Hilda Solis’ constant habit of deleting negative or critical comments from her Instagram page, almost as if she would rather pretend critics don’t exist rather than answer for her actions.  On March 4 th , when the Trump Administration barred abortion providers from receiving federal funding, Solis stated “The Trump Administration’s latest attack on women’s health diminishes the sanctity of the doctor-patient relationship and impacts the quality of healthcare accessed by four million people throughout the country”.  Yet she wishes to do the very same (diminish the sanctity of the doctor-patient relationship) by imposing herself as the chief medical decision maker for LA County Employees and residents by requiring them to undergo a medical procedure, the COVID-19 vaccine by way of coercion. What is further contradictory is that the LA County Board of Supervisor's "Policy of Equity", which can be found on the County Equity Oversight Panel website states the following: "The County WILL NOT tolerate unlawful discrimination on the basis of age; ancestry; color; ethnicity; religious creed; ...; disability; marital status; MEDICAL CONDITION; ...; or any other characteristic protected by state of federal law." Further, "as a preventive measure, the County also will not tolerate inappropriate conduct toward others based on a protected status even if the conduct does not meet the legal 

definition of discrimination or unlawful harassment. All County employees are responsible for conducting themselves in accordance with this Policy and its associated Procedures."  This section is preceded by the statement "The Los Angeles County Policy of Equity reflects and builds upon our MISSION, VISION, and VALUES, which each employee is responsible for demonstrating in both ACTIONS AND WORDS". The very fact that the board is considering requiring individuals to be vaccinated in order to participate in society displays that the Board is willing to act contradictory to its MISSION, VISION, and VALUES, and does in fact condone discrimination- discrimination against those who have decided for themselves that the COVID-19 vaccine is not the right medical choice for themselves. 

The Board is willing to discriminate against grown, functioning, free Americans, who have decided that the repeated contradictions surrounding the vaccine have caused them not to trust it enough to let it enter their body.  The county is willing to discriminate against those who wonder why, after being told repeatedly by government and public health officials, large corporations, big tech, and countless other bodies that the vaccine is ONLY "Safe and Effective", they are simultaneously being told "the virus can still spread, masks must still be worn", what benefit remains to outrisk a blood clot, unknown reaction, or the unknown long-term effects.  The governor of California has called this "A Pandemic of the Unvaccinated", yet the unvaccinated seem to be no better off than the vaccinated at this point.  At this point, it now appears that there is no benefit to being vaccinated besides being free from threat, not from the virus but from tyrannical policy.  Any foreign substance being introduced into the body can present a risk, whether small or large.  If those who are otherwise healthy and have little chance of infection by the virus, and now have a wide range of treatments available to them to treat an infection, and have a high likelihood of getting through any infection by the virus without great harm to themselves decide that they’d rather not introduce a new substance into their body from a manufacturer that has a history of lawsuits (and one of those manufacturers holding the record for the largest criminal settlement ever awarded in history), developed under a presidential administration many did not trust in record time for the chance that it may avert what turns out for them to be a little worse than the flu they had before, is that outrageous? If their risk to transmit to others is at the same level of those who have already taken the shot(s), as we’ve discovered in the previous weeks, is it honest to say that forcing them to receive the same shot is for the best interest of the public?  This very agenda item displays that this is not a pandemic of the unvaccinated, but a pandemic of bad leadership- a pandemic of policymakers who recklessly, and without suffering consequence of their own policy, demand control over the sanctity of their constituents’ own bodies under the veil of “saving lives”.  How can anyone on this board think you are uniquely qualified to “save lives” when you haven’t even demonstrated your ability to save money, electricity, or a small business? 

This state has not learned from any bad decision it has made during this pandemic.  It has demonstrated that it is not careful, nor considerate to the rights of its Citizens by any measure of the imagination.  You have locked our children out of schools and inside their homes for a year, while allowing adult-appearing children to destroy parts of our cities for days on end, and while law abiding citizens were locked in their homes under curfew.  You have kept healthy people out of fitness facilities while allowing liquor stores and smoke shops to remain open.  You closed small businesses from serving their customers without proving anywhere near beyond a reasonable doubt that they were a danger to them, while allowing the homeless to setup shop on sidewalks, riverbeds, and parks.  You arrested people for going to the beach while you released dangerous criminals from prisons, and now you are considering requiring County employees to take a vaccine that does not 

inhibit the risk of infecting others, and considering banning otherwise healthy people from utilizing indoor public spaces for the same. Above all else, the board simply lacks the authority, moral or otherwise, to require someone to put something into their body against their will, in addition to lacking the authority to retaliate, segregate, or discriminate against them by withholding access to indoor facilities, or any right by any means for their refusal to do so. 

The decision to take a vaccine should not be made under duress, threat or fear, because everyone is telling you to get it, or that you won’t be able to participate in certain things without it, that you will not have the same rights as others without it.  It should simply be, as with any other medical procedure, that the person freely and willfully chooses to because they believe or are convinced that it is the best decision for themselves. Now I am facing the same oppressive measures here that are being implemented in Italy. 

I would like to ask you to oppose items S-1 and 73-B, which were discussed in your Board of Supervisors meeting this morning (August 10th). Please allow each citizen to freely decide whether to get the Covid injection. This is a huge decision; this shot is still experimental, and people need to be able to freely choose whether they feel comfortable putting this inadequately tested and unprecedented gene technology into their bodies. 

On average, it takes 10-15  years  to study and approve a vaccine, but these injections were trialed and authorized in a mere 7-9 months. During the testing phase, the researchers did not complete the animal trials before they started the human trials, and the people who are now getting this injection are the guinea pigs. The pharmaceutical companies have indemnity, meaning that they can not be held liable when a person becomes vaccine-damaged. If they are not liable, is  SBCC  willing to take the blame when people report vaccine injuries and deaths? The  long-term  consequences of the COVID-19 injections are also completely unknown. Every day the number of adverse reaction reports and vaccine-related deaths on VAERS (Vaccine Adverse Event Reporting System)  is growing significantly, and 31,389 Covid-19 injection fatalities have been reported in the USA and UK this past year. VAERS is a government website:  https://vaers.hhs.gov/ Even after these injections are approved for standard use, the decision to get them should still always be up to the individual. Informed consent is essential and must be protected! Forced vaccination is repressive and embodies totalitarianism, not liberty and justice for all. It is anti-American that private businesses and public places that are publicly funded with our tax dollars should not be open and available to ALL. 

In September of 2020 the CDC statistic that the Covid-19 death rate is under 0.5% for everyone but people over 70. The death rate for the Delta variant is extremely low. I do not see that this statistic warrants the draconian implementation of mandatory  vaccination. Furthermore, there are many  natural, alternative ways  of treating and preventing Covid, such as Ivermectin, Quercetin, high doses of Zinc and Vitamin C and D, healthy diet and exercise that are concealed by Big Pharma because public knowledge of this would be a threat to their profits and a threat to their Emergency 

Use Authorization of the injection. A lot of the statistics and studies being released about the injections in mainstream media and in the government are being funded by the pharmaceutical companies themselves, so they have a  self-interest in the  matter. I have traveled in 14 states and 6 countries since the start of the pandemic and have always been healthy. I have been on planes, trains, in hotels, and with friends and family and have never noticed that anyone that I was with was sick. Let me ask you this: If we didn't constantly have the TV and news scaring us with Covid "asymptomatic" and "numbers" and "cases", would you even know there was a pandemic? How many people do you yourself personally know who have died of Covid who did not have co-morbidities? The real pandemic is the constant fear propaganda being pumped through our televisions and radios day and night. 

My brother had Covid and was sick for 2 days. My aunt was sick for 5 days. My landlord was sick for 6 days. I have not met anyone who was sick for longer than 6 days. None of this warrants giving up the freedoms that our country was founded on! Would you eat something that contained these ingredients?

Subject: Add my coment to the board meeting august 10th-73-B. Evaluation of COVID-19 Vaccine Requirements for Indoor Public Spaces 

## 73-B. Evaluation of COVID-19 Vaccine Requirements for Indoor Public Spaces 

OPPOSE In regards to agenda meeting -73-B. Evaluation of COVID-19 Vaccine Requirements for Indoor Public Spaces 

The Unruh civil rights act prohibits what you are trying to allow businesses to do.Get ready for the lawsuits The Unruh Civil Rights Act (colloquially the "Unruh Act") is an expansive 1950s California law that prohibits any business in California from engaging in unlawful discrimination against all persons (consumers) within California's jurisdiction, where the unlawful discrimination is in part based on a person's sex, race, color, religion, ancestry, national origin, age, disability, medical condition, genetic information, marital status, sexual orientation, citizenship, primary language, or immigration status. [1][2][3] The Unruh Act applies to ALL businesses in California including: hotels and motels, restaurants, theaters, hospitals, barber and beauty shops, housing accommodations, and retail establishments.[4] The law was enacted in 1959 and was named for its author, Jesse M. Unruh. The Unruh Civil Rights Act is codified as California Civil Code section 51.[3][5] What do you think " medical condition " equates too? The California Supreme Court has repeatedly "interpreted the [law] as protecting classes other than those listed on its face".[6] For example, even prior to the 2005 addition of sexual orientation to the law's list of covered classes, the Unruh Act had been "construed as protecting gays and lesbians from arbitrary discrimination",[6] such as in the case of Rolon v. Kulwitzky.[7] 

-Ashley Johnson From: ExecutiveOffice

Honorable Janice Hahn

Via email 

Re: Motion to require vaccination checks before business entry ; retail exemption 

Dear Honorable Supervisor Hahn :

I am writing in response to your motion which would require vaccination checks at the point of entry at many 

private businesses, including retail and respectfully request your consideration i n removing all retail from the 

vaccine verification ordinance. Unlike other industries such as restaurants or fitness centers, retail store 

customers and employees can always keep a mask on, and retailers will continue to strongly encourage and 

incentiviz e our employees and our customers to get vaccinated as well as wear a face covering while it the 

retail establishment. 

The California Retailers Association is the only statewide trade association representing all segments of the 

retail industry including general merchandise, department stores, mass merchandisers, restaurants, 

convenience stores, supermarkets and grocery stores, chain drug, and specialty retail s uch as auto, vision, 

jewelry, hardware, and home stores. CRA works on behalf of California’s retail industry, which operates over 

400,000 retail establishments with a gross domestic product of billions of dollars annually and employs 

millions of Californi ans and many Angelinos as Los Angeles is the largest retail market in the state. 

Our retail members have expressed significant concerns about the prospect of requiring their employees to 

“police” their customers for v accine status at the doors of their businesses. Enforcing mask mandates has 

been difficult in many cases – checking vaccination cards will stir much more emotion among patrons. Placing 

our employees at the center of this conflict is not something most of our employees are trained to do and is 

worrisome for their personal well -being. 

Retailers are strong supporters of the COVID -19 vaccinations and were some of the first to work with the 

County and City to help with the vaccination rollout - offering our services, facilities, and expertise to get the 

vaccine out as safely and effectively as possible. We certainly share concerns with the increased spread of the 

Delta variant and the number of people who are not yet vaccinated. 

As to your motion requiring vaccination checks at business entry points, we have some immediate questions 

we would like clarified prior to the drafting and adoption of any ordinance :

o What constitutes “proof” of vaccination? Each vaccination facility provided diff erent forms of

May 2021 

Memo on COVID Vaccination Mandates 

Liberty Counsel is a national nonprofit litigation, education and public policy organizati on 

advancing religious freedom, the sanctity of human life, and family . We have engaged in 

extensive litigation regarding civil rights violations ostensibly justified by “COVID -19 .” We have 

had great success on behalf of our many clients at circu it courts of appeal and at the United 

States Supreme Court . See, e.g., Harvest Rock Church v. Newsom , No. 20A137, 2021 WL 

406257 (U.S. Feb. 5, 2021); Harvest Rock Church v. Newsom, Gov. of CA , No. 20A94, 2020 

WL 7061630 (U.S. Dec. 3, 2020); Elim Romanian P entecostal Church v. Pritzker, 962 F.3d 341 

(7th Cir. 2020); Maryville Baptist Church, Inc. v. Beshear , 957 F.3d 610 (6th Cir. 2020). The 

existence of COVID -19 does not justify the numerous violations of fundamental individual, 

economic and religious liber ties. These include the rights of personal autonomy and bodily 

integrity, and the right to accept or reject the various COVID vaccines based on religious belief 

or other grounds. 

COVID Vaccines Cannot Be Mandatory Under Emergency Use Authorization 

On the issue of the COVID vaccines : a ll of such have been released under an Emergency 

Use Authorization (“EUA”) and employers (religious and non -religious alike) may not condition 

continued employment on taking an EUA -authorized COVID vaccin e. 

The COVID vaccines are in a special category and cannot be treated like FDA licensed 

vaccines. None of the COVID vaccines are FDA licensed; nor have they received full FDA 

approval. Rather, their approval is under the special provision noted above as EUA. This means 

that there is not enough data (which includes duration of testing) for the FDA to render a final 

approval. More importantly, no one, including private employers, may coerce individuals (by 

threatening their employment or otherwise) to take an EUA vaccine. Federal law requires full 

and informed, voluntary consent. 

All employees – whether employed by religious or ganizations, or not – are protected 

against mandated COVID -19 vaccines, under  21 U.S.C. §360bbb -3,  which provides that EUA 

products (like all of these vaccines) require (as a condition of emergency approval) that people 

have “the option to accept or refuse administration of the product .” “FDA has an obligation 

to ensure that recipients of the vaccine under an EUA are informed… that they have the option 

to accept or refuse the vaccine .” 1 (emphasis added). There is no exception in the statute for 

“private employers” as opposed to government ; or for religious or non -religious employers . All 

EUA vaccines are optional.     

> 1https://www.eeoc.gov/wysk/what -you -should -know -about -covid -19 -and -ada -rehabilitation -act -and -other -eeo -laws COVID vaccination mandate s
> May 11 , 2021
> Page 2

Moreover, a t t he Centers for Disease Control Advisory Committee on Immunization 

Practices (ACIP) meeting held in August 2020, CDC -ACIP Executive Secretary Amanda Cohn, 

MD confirmed the non -mandatory nature of an EUA vaccine: “under an Emergency Use 

Authorization, an EUA, va ccines are not allowed to be mandatory . So, early in this 

vaccination phase, individuals will have to be consented and they won ’t be able to be mandated. ”

(emphasis added). 2

COVID Vaccines Cannot Be Mandatory Under Title VII 

In general, employee vaccine religious exemption requests must be accommodated, 

where a reasonable accommodation exists without undue hardship to the employer, under Title 

VII of the Civil Rights Act of 1964. 

Many people hold sincere religious beliefs ag ainst taking any vaccines, or taking those 

derived from aborted fetal cell lines, or taking those sold by companies that profit from the sale 

of vaccines and other products derived from abortion .

Title VII, as amended, prohibits two categories of employm ent practices. It is unlawful for 

an employer: “(1) to fail or refuse to hire or to discharge any individual, or otherwise to 

discriminate against any individual with respect to his compensation, terms, conditions, 

or privileges of employment , because of s uch individual’s race, color, religion , sex, or national 

origin; or (2) to limit, segregate, or classify his employees or applicants for employment in any 

way which would deprive or tend to deprive any individual of employment opportunities or 

otherwise ad versely affect his status as an employee, because of such individual’s race, 

color, religion , sex, or national origin.” 42 U.S.C. § 2000e –2(a). (Emphasis added). 

By pattern and practice, virtually every employer in America has shown that reasonable 

accom modations and alternatives to vaccination indeed exist for employees, and these have 

been required all along since the inception of COVID : self -screening with temperature checks, 

wearing personal protective equipment (PPE) , and complying with other safety protocols until 

the number of COVID infections work their way down to acceptable levels. Logically, if these 

measures are and were effective at preventing the spread of COVID, they will continue to be 

effective. Thus, no employer can claim an undue hardshi p by allowing employees to do what 

they have been doing for over a year, in the alternative to a vaccine. 

Liberty Counsel’s interpretation of Title VII is also supported by the footnoted, linked 

press releases from the Equal Employment Opportunity Commiss ion (EEOC) 3 and the U.S. 

Department of Justice (US DOJ) .4 It is unlawful for employers to force vaccinations on staff and 

employees holding religious convictions against a vaccine , and to refuse a reasonable 

accommodation . This goes for healthcare industry employers . In 2018, one hospital paid 

$89,000 to settle a suit after refusing to accommodate and firing employees who declined flu 

vaccinations based on their religious beliefs. US DOJ sought compensatory damages on behalf 

of a nursing home employee against whom Ozaukee County, Wisconsin discriminated .

Singl ing Out Employees For Individual Questioning or Adverse Action 

Under the ADA , GINA and Title VII 

In interpreting the Americans With Disabilities Act (“ADA”), t he EEOC has op ined that it

is improper to “ask only one employee —as opposed to asking all employees —questions 

designed to determine if [he] has COVID -19, or require that this employee alone have [his] 

temperature taken or undergo other screening or testing[.] “If an emp loyer wishes to ask only a 

particular employee to answer such questions, or to have [his] temperature taken or undergo 

other screening or testing, the ADA requires the employer to have a reasonable belief based on 

objective evidence that this person might have the disease. ”5 EEOC has also stated that an 

employer ma y not “ ask an employee who is physically coming into the workplace whether 

they have family members who have COVID -19 or symptoms associated with COVID -19 ,” 

as such is a violation of the Genetic Information Nondiscrimination Act (GINA). 6

The Occupational Safety and Health Administration (OSHA) has also recognized that 

there is “not evidence that COVID -19 vaccines prevent transmission of the virus from 

person -to -person ,” and reiterated that employers should not improperly distinguish between 

employees. 7 (Emphasis added). “The most effective COVID -19 prevention programs … include the 

following elements:” 

15. Not distinguishing between workers who are vaccinated and those who 

are not: Workers who are vaccinated must continue to follow protective measures, 

such as wearing a face covering and remaining physically distant, because at this 

time, there is not evidence that COVID -19 vaccines prevent transmission of the 

virus from person -to -person. The  CDC explains  that experts need to understand 

more about the protection that COVID -19 vaccines provide before deciding to 

change recommendations on steps everyone should take to slow the spread of the 

virus that causes COVID -19 .8 (Emphasis original and added ). 

On May 22, 2021 , OSHA ’s reversed the following guidance regarding whether adverse 

reactions experienced by employe es who take the shot under certain conditions or arrangements 

are recordable on OSHA’s recordkeeping log 9:

If I require my employees to take the COVID -19 vaccine as a condition of their 

employment, are adverse reactions to the vaccine recordable? 

If you require your employees to be vaccinated as a condition of employment (i.e., 

for work -related reasons), then any adverse reaction to the COVID -19 vaccine 

is work -related . The adverse reaction is recordable if it is a new case under 29 

CFR 1904.6 and meets one or more of the general recording criteria in 29 CFR 

1904.7. 10 (Emphasis added). 

I do not require my employees to get the COVID -19 vaccine. However, I do 

recommend that they receive the vaccine and may provide it to them or make 

ar rangements for them to receive it offsite. If an employee has an adverse 

reaction to the vaccine, am I required to record it? 

No. Although adverse reactions to recommended COVID -19 vaccines may be 

recordable under 29 CFR 1904.4(a) if the reaction is: (1) w ork -related, (2) a new 

case, and (3) meets one or more of the general recording criteria in 29 CFR 1904.7, 

OSHA is exercising its enforcement discretion to only require the recording of 

adverse effects to required vaccines at this time. Therefore, you do n ot need to 

record adverse effects from COVID -19 vaccines that you recommend, but do

not require . (Emphasis added). 

Note that for this discretion to apply, the vaccine must be truly voluntary . For 

example, an employee’s choice to accept or reject the vacci ne cannot affect their 

performance rating or professional advancement. An employee who chooses not 

to receive the vaccine cannot suffer any repercussions from this choice. If 

employees are not free to choose whether or not to receive the vaccine 

without fe aring adverse action, then the vaccine is not merely 

“recommended” and employers should consult the above FAQ regarding COVID -

19 vaccines that are a condition of employment. (Emphasis added). 11 

On May 22, 2021, OSHA’s updated FAQ stated as follows: 

Are adverse reactions to the COVID -19 vaccine recordable on the OSHA 

recordkeeping log? 

DOL and OSHA, as well as other federal agencies, are working diligently to 

encourage COVID -19 vaccinations. OSHA does not wish to have any appearance 

of discouraging workers from receiving COVID -19 vaccination, and also does not 

wish to disincentivize employe rs’ vaccination efforts. As a result, OSHA will not 

enforce 29 CFR 1904’s recording requirements to require any employers to record 

worker side effects from COVID -19 vaccination through May 2022. We will 

reevaluate the agency’s position at that time to det ermine the best course of action 

moving forward. 12 

Finally, questioning employees (much less taking adverse employment action against 

them) on the basis of church membership or church attendance potentially violates not only the 

ADA, but also Title VII, w hich prohibits discrimination based on religious worship or religious 

practices engaged in by the employee outside the workplace. 

Conclusion 

There are strong protections under federal law for persons who wish to decline the 

current EUA -authorized COVID vaccines. Neither government nor private employers may force 

anyone to receive any of the COVID injections.

To: The Los Angeles County Board of Supervisors 

From: M. Williams, District 4 

RE: Agenda Item 73 -B for 8/10/2021 Meeting 

This serves as written public comment concerning Agenda Item 73 -B for the Board meeting of 

August 10, 2021. 

With all due respect, this is a misguided motion. There are many reasons that are personal to 

each person for why they choose to forego the Pfizer, ModeRNA or J&J injection (s) . To severely 

restrict those persons who have chosen to forego the inject ion(s) at this time is punitive and 

persecutorial, and it inflicts severe hardship on single and single -parent households especially. 

The Board Members speak of using an equity lens, being inclusive and having an anti -racis m

policy, but I submit to you th at the se proposed severe restriction s will have the greatest 

negative impa ct on communities of color, who according to your own data are the least 

vaccinated population in the county. The people of these communities are without 

generational wealth, hard assets and trust funds. They don’t make the six figure salaries that 

you do , and most don’t have savings and pension funds , that you no doubt enjoy. They work 

mostly hourly and paycheck to paycheck. A fter the last year and a half that all of us have 

end ured , many have already lost so much , and many anticipate the loss of their housing when 

the eviction moratorium ends. The people who will most be losing jobs, housing and 

business es because of this motion will be these very same peopl e you claim to care about .

I have listened to s everal of the Board Members calling people names, speak ing negatively and 

derogatorily of those who have not been injected . Y ou blame them for the increased spread of 

the disease. But you don’t know their reasons . For instance, d o you know what it’s like to be 

victimized by medical error by medical professionals and there is no accountability, resources 

or recourse to address that. Do you know what it’s like as a dependent, to have a sol e parent 

go into a hospital walking and talking for a minor routine procedure and be sent back on a 

stretcher paralyzed and unable to speak for more than a decade until death because of medical 

malpractice. I doubt you do. And do you know the impact and trauma of going to the hospital 

to be treated for a health issue that flared up only to have a medical professional improperly 

administer the needle and medicine which resulted in necrosis to that part of the body and 

necessitated prosthetic replacements. Somehow, I doubt any of you do. These are traumatic 

events. 

And it really doesn’t help to say that whatever adverse reactions there may be are rare. 

Nobody want s to be that “rare” case —the exception to what normally happens, especially 

when there is no recourse, relief or help with whatever that rarity turns out to be . If you are your sole support for yourself or your children, it is not a risk to take lightly because you, as that 

rare victim, and those children, are stuck with that outcome for the res t of your life. Will you 

be left a virtual vegetable? Will you be left requiring some sort of assisted care for the rest of 

your life? Where will the money for any medical attention to address it come from ? These are 

not things to take lightly or rush headlong into simply because other people want you to do it. 

What one person can risk, another cannot. No one can decide fo r another person, what that 

other person can risk or not risk for his/her life .

Other people aren’t going to take care of you, a nd no one has the right to demand through 

coercion what someone else must have injected into them under penalty of the loss of their 

job, their housing , their livelihood. That is wrong! Those who are not injected do not value 

their lives any less than an yone else, but they are also not to blame for what is happening. And 

it is destructive , divisive and mentally traumatizing to blame them for what is happening. it’s 

just plain wrong! 

This item represents a cruel and beastly direction the Board is taking , and it will inflict great and 

inequitable harm on those communities of color who are doing the best they can just to 

survive. If you take away their ability to work, the ir liberty to go about do ing things they need ,

to do freely , and their ability to enjoy the simple pleasures of life, you leave them with no way 

to live and no way out. It will r esult in unnecessary additional mental anguish, depression and 

suicides because you have taken away all hope. In effect you have taken from them their ri ght 

to life, liberty and the pursuit of happiness . It is beastly cruel and heartless and all because 

those who are injected want to return to their life of eating, drinking, merrymaking and 

unmasking. This is wrong !

I will leave you with one last though t. At the start of the pandemic no one was injected —not 

patients nor medical professionals. Now, the same medical professionals who worked so hard 

and bore the greatest burden of this pandemic are given an ultimatum (from the governor). 

That is ungratef ul, unappreciative and a slap in their face. More than that, it is shortsighted 

when long before the pandemic there was a shortage of nurses and other medical staff 

especially in the hard -hit communities of color. Now, with this move, the shortage will b e

greater and which community do you think will be the most negatively impacted by it. As Dr. 

Ghaly had said before, it’s not a matter of not having the beds, but not having enough staffing 

for the beds. So , getting rid of badly needed and experienced me dical staff is a very bad 

decision. 

I urge you NOT to pass this motion but to unanimously vote No on it. 

M. Williams

NOTICE FOR EMPLOYERS, UNIVERSITIES AND OTHER INSTITUTIONS MANDATING COVID-19 VACCINES 

Revised 6/4/21 

This serves as notice that the requirement for any individual to be vaccinated against COVID-19 for employment or participation at a university or other institution violates federal law. All COVID-19 vaccines are merely authorized, not approved or licensed, by the federal government; they are Emergency Use Authorization (EUA) only. They merely “may be effective.” Federal law states: 

Title 21 U.S.C. § 360bbb-3(e)(1)(A)(ii)(I-III) of the Federal Food, Drug, and Cosmetic Act states: 

individuals to whom the product is administered are informed— (I) that the Secretary has authorized the emergency use of the product; (II) of the significant known and potential benefits and risks of such use, and of the extent to which such benefits and risks are unknown; and 

(III) of the option to accept or refuse administration of the product, of the consequences, if any, of refusing administration of the product, and of the alternatives to the product that are available and of their benefits and risks. 

EUA products are by definition experimental and thus require the right to refuse. Under the Nuremberg Code, the foundation of ethical medicine, no one may be coerced to participate in a medical experiment. Consent of the individual is “absolutely essential.” A federal court held that the U.S. military could not mandate EUA vaccines to soldiers. Doe #1 v. Rumsfeld, 297 F.Supp.2d 119 (2003). The court held: "...the United States cannot demand that members of the armed forces also serve as guinea pigs for experimental drugs." Id. at 135. No court has ever upheld a mandate for an EUA vaccine. The liability for forced participation in a medical experiment, including injury or death, may be incalculable. Medical and religious exemptions will be insufficient to overcome the illegality of EUA vaccine mandates. Children’s Health Defense urges U.S. employers, universities and other institutions to respect and uphold the rights of individuals to refuse EUA COVID-19 vaccines.  

> This notice is adapted from materials at Health Freedom Defense Fund, https://healthfreedomdefense.org/
> 1227 North Peachtree Parkway, Suite 1202. Peachtree City, GA 30269 www.childrenshealthdefense.org

Dear whom it may concern, 

May this letter find you all in good health and spirit. Greetings, my name is

Protection of previous SARS-CoV-2 infection is similar to that of BNT162b2 vaccine protection: A three-month nationwide experience from Israel 

Yair Goldberg  1*, Micha Mandel  2, Yonatan Woodbridge 3 , Ronen Fluss 3, Ilya Novikov 3,Rami Yaari 3, Arnona Ziv 3, Laurence Freedman 3, Amit Huppert 3, 41Technion - Israel Institute of Technology, Israel 2The Hebrew University of Jerusalem, Israel 3The Gertner Institute for Epidemiology & Health Policy Research, Sheba Medical Center, Israel 4Tel Aviv University, Israel 

>  

Contributed equally *corresponding author email: yairgo@technion.ac.il 

Abstract 

Worldwide shortage of vaccination against SARS-CoV-2 infection while the pandemic is still uncontrolled leads many states to the dilemma whether or not to vaccinate previously infected persons. Understanding the level of protection of previous infection compared to that of vaccination is critical for policy making. We analyze an updated individual-level database of the entire population of Israel to assess the protection efficacy of both prior infection and vaccination in preventing subsequent SARS-CoV-2 infection, hospitalization with COVID-19, severe disease, and death due to COVID-19. Vaccination was highly effective with overall estimated efficacy for documented infection of 92·8% (CI:[92·6, 93·0]); hospitalization 94·2% (CI:[93·6, 94·7]); severe illness 94·4% (CI:[93·6, 95·0]); and death 93·7% (CI:[92·5, 94·7]). Similarly, the overall estimated level of protection from prior SARS-CoV-2 infection for documented infection is 94·8% (CI:[94·4, 95·1]); hospitalization 94·1% (CI:[91·9, 95·7]); and severe illness 96·4% (CI:[92·5, 98·3]). Our results question the need to vaccinate previously-infected individuals.

Keywords : vaccine efficacy, COVID-19, SARS-CoV-2, previous infection, protection from reinfection 

Introduction 

Israel is currently in the later stages of a vaccination campaign to reduce both SARS-CoV-2 infection and the number of COVID-19 cases. Israel is administering the BNT162b2 vaccine, developed by BioNTech in cooperation with Pfizer, 1 for which an Emergency Use Authorization (EUA) was issued by the Food and Drug Administration (FDA). 2 The vaccine is administered in two doses, with a 21-day interval between doses. Israel launched its COVID-19 vaccination program on December 20, 2020. The vaccine became available, free of charge, to different risk groups in stages: first to those older than 60 years old, nursing home residents, healthcare workers, and patients with severe comorbidities, and then gradually to younger age groups. As of February 6, 2021, the vaccine was made available to all individuals aged 16 or older not previously infected by SARS-CoV-2. As of March 20, 2021, 77% of the eligible population is vaccinated. Due to the high caseload and the local detection of viral mutants such as B.1.1.7, Israel went into a third nationwide lockdown during the vaccination campaign. A light lockdown began on December 24, 2020, and was tightened on January 5, 2021. Restrictions were eased in stages starting February 7, 2021. The dynamics of the epidemic as well as the vaccination campaign appear in Figure 1. SARS-CoV-2 testing in Israel is carried out according to the following policy: individuals may request testing due to either symptoms or contact with an individual who tested positive. These PCR tests are given free of charge. Individuals who have come into contact with an individual who tested positive are required to self-quarantine for 14 days. This quarantine period may be shortened to 10 days if the individual is tested twice during the first 10 days, and both test results are negative. Individuals who have received both vaccine doses, and had the second dose seven days or more before a contact with a positive individual, and do not have symptoms, are not required to self-quarantine, and thus have 

> All rights reserved. No reuse allowed without permission. (which was not certified by peer review) is the author/funder, who has granted medRxiv a license to display the preprint in perpetuity. The copyright holder for this preprint this version posted April 24, 2021. ;https://doi.org/10.1101/2021.04.20.21255670 doi: medRxiv preprint

3less motivation to get tested. In addition to voluntary testing, Israel conducts routine testing of all nursing-home workers. Recent results based on aggregated data 3–5 and individual level data 6–10 have shown that the vaccine substantially reduces the number of severe COVID-19 cases. Two studies also indicate that the viral load of vaccinated individuals is significantly reduced. 11,12 These encouraging initial results are based on a short follow-up of vaccinated individuals. Results on previous COVID-19 infection 13–16 suggest protection against reinfection compared to uninfected unvaccinated individuals. In this study, we estimate the efficacy of the vaccine in the reduction of documented SARS-CoV-2 infection and severe COVID-19 disease. We focus on four cohorts: unvaccinated individuals; vaccinated individuals followed from first dose to a week after the second dose; vaccinated individuals followed from a week after the second dose onwards, and the Recovered Cohort of unvaccinated individuals previously infected with SARS-CoV-2. For more details, see the Methods section. All efficacies, of vaccine or previous infection, are compared to the unvaccinated cohort. The prospective observational analysis that we present faced several challenges. The first challenge was self-selection of treatment, which implies differences in potential risk factors between vaccinated and non-vaccinated individuals. These include age, sex, socio-demographic level, 17 level of infection in the immediate environment, and possibly other behavioral variables that could affect level of exposure to the virus. The second challenge was detection bias: willingness to undergo vaccination can be associated with trust in the healthcare system, which may also imply a tendency to comply with testing regulations. On the other hand, vaccinated individuals may feel more protected and may ignore mild symptoms indicative of the disease, and have less motivation to get tested as they are not required to self-quarantine after a contact with a positive individual. The third challenge was the variation in infection risk throughout the vaccination campaign, mainly due to varying lockdown levels, relative prevalence of viral mutants, and local outbreaks. Lastly, the status of individuals (i.e., unvaccinated, partially vaccinated, or fully vaccinated) was
